Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The computer_vision variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 297 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 601 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 297 + O(1/n^601). Summing over all modes and applying the computer_vision convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
